        "content": "Web search results for query: \"Electron React TypeScript best practices 2025\"\n\nI'll search for the latest best practices for using Electron with React and TypeScript in 2025.\n\nLinks: [{\"title\":\"React with TypeScript | Electron Forge\",\"url\":\"https://www.electronforge.io/guides/framework-integration/react-with-typescript\"},{\"title\":\"Create Desktop Apps with Electron, React, and TypeScript\",\"url\":\"https://www.freecodecamp.org/news/create-desktop-apps-with-electron-react-and-typescript/\"},{\"title\":\"Build an Electron app with Typescript and React\",\"url\":\"https://www.codiga.io/blog/build-electron-typescript-react-app/\"},{\"title\":\"Mastering TypeScript Best Practices to Follow in 2025\",\"url\":\"https://www.bacancytechnology.com/blog/typescript-best-practices\"},{\"title\":\"Using Electron With React | React Resources\",\"url\":\"https://reactresources.com/topics/electron\"},{\"title\":\"reactjs - How to set up Electron with React and TypeScript? - Stack Overflow\",\"url\":\"https://stackoverflow.com/questions/47316787/how-to-set-up-electron-with-react-and-typescript\"},{\"title\":\"Build a cross-platform desktop app with electron and react typescript, tailwind css - DEV Community\",\"url\":\"https://dev.to/phamquyetthang/build-a-cross-platform-desktop-app-with-electron-and-react-typescript-tailwind-css-39k0\"},{\"title\":\"React Best Practices You Should Follow for Development\",\"url\":\"https://www.devacetech.com/insights/react-best-practices\"},{\"title\":\"33 React Best Practices For 2025 | Technostacks\",\"url\":\"https://technostacks.com/blog/react-best-practices/\"},{\"title\":\"GitHub - yhirose/react-typescript-electron-sample-with-create-react-app-and-electron-builder: React-TypeScript-Electron sample with Create React App and Electron Builder\",\"url\":\"https://github.com/yhirose/react-typescript-electron-sample-with-create-react-app-and-electron-builder\"}]\n\nBased on my search results, here are the best practices for using Electron with React and TypeScript in 2025:\n\n## Project Setup and Boilerplates\n\nReact Electron Boilerplate is highly recommended because it has everything needed out of the box: community support, regular updates, and comprehensive documentation. It's ideal for using React, TypeScript and Electron together.\n\nElectron-forge is another viable option that works conveniently, though installing React TypeScript and Tailwind CSS requires some initial setup time.\n\n## Core TypeScript Best Practices\n\n### 1. Enable Strict Mode\nType-checking is crucial and should be enabled by activating strict mode in your tsconfig.json file. This ensures TypeScript executes at a higher safety level, enhances code reliability, and prevents common mistakes like undefined values and null.\n\n### 2. Use TypeScript for Both Processes\nTypeScript support should be implemented for both the Electron main process source code and the renderer process.\n\n### 3. Type Safety with Interfaces\nDefine interfaces for your data types and use them consistently across components for clean, scalable code.\n\n## React-Specific Best Practices\n\n### 1. Functional Components and Hooks\nUse functional components and hooks to simplify code, improve readability, and reduce complexity. They replace class components and lifecycle methods, making state and side-effect management more intuitive and maintainable.\n\n### 2. Performance Optimization\nOptimize performance with React.memo and useMemo, and write reusable, modular components.\n\n### 3. Project Structure\nTo make your React app more scalable, organize code by feature or type, extract logic into custom hooks and utilities, use TypeScript for type safety, create reusable components, stick to clear naming conventions, and use a consistent folder structure.\n\n## Electron-Specific Configuration\n\n### 1. JSX Configuration\nAdd \"jsx\": \"react\" to the tsconfig.json and rename your files to .tsx instead of .ts for TypeScript to support React out of the box.\n\n### 2. Entry Points\nThe webpack entry point should be ./src/index.tsx while electron's entry point is ./index.js. Electron loads index.js that loads index.html that loads ./dist/bundle.js.\n\n## Modern Features and Tools\n\n### 1. ES6+ Features\nPrioritize clean code principles and modern ES6+ features for better scalability, maintainability, quality, and flexibility.\n\n### 2. Testing Strategy\nUse React Testing Library to simulate real user behavior and focus on testing behavior rather than implementation details.\n\n### 3. State Management\nUse local state for component-specific data and Context API for shared state. For complex apps, adopt libraries like Redux or Zustand, avoiding overuse of global state.\n\n## Development Workflow\n\n### 1. Hot Reloading\nSet up development mode where the Electron app reloads when you make edits, displaying lint errors in the console.\n\n### 2. Build and Distribution\nUse Electron Builder to package your app for production distribution.\n\n### 3. Documentation\nThorough documentation ensures seamless development and easier team collaboration.\n\nThese best practices emphasize type safety, modern React patterns, proper project structure, and efficient development workflows to create maintainable and scalable Electron applications with React and TypeScript in 2025."
      }
    ]
